# mindset
_1899 claims from 474 videos, 2020-01-18 → 2025-04-28; merged into 1873 distinct claims. Script output (reduce.py), not edited by hand._

Line format: `- [Nv Y1-Y2 types] claim {numbers by month} cites` — Nv = distinct videos that said it; types P principle, R prescription, O opinion, A anecdote, D data; `?` = low-confidence caption; `also:` = differently-worded variants merged into it.

## severity / restlessnes / weaker / inside (40)
- [1v 2020 O] People who were chubby as kids tend to chase a shredded, striated physique, while small kids like him chase a massive, muscular one. {{J608dwN-uqY@0}}
- [1v 2020 D] He cites a study suggesting expansive, space-taking postures raise testosterone in men; owning your space in the gym matters, which crowded commercial gyms prevent. {{smAsuyqAQOo@315}}
- [1v 2020 O] Treat the hardgainer label as an indication of what to do, not a curse; you just need to be extra diligent with diet. {{6nKKGsCCqxo@189}}

- [1v 2020 D] Verbal commitments increase follow-through; studies show people who commit out loud are more likely to act {{JXVJtNrIZ9U@540}}

- [1v 2020 P] Strike a middle ground between impulsiveness and procrastination: tolerate some anxiety so you can prepare and act precisely, but make the moment happen rather than waiting for it {{xoxYiiTPdIs@62}}

- [1v 2021 O] Restlessness usually means you are not working; once you work, restlessness goes away {{GJudkSxdp-Q@1040}}
- [1v 2021 O] Being very busy can lead to dissociation, where you no longer fully recognise yourself in the mirror; people who go off the rails often forgot who they were {{tLvshnh6vZA@1026}}

- [1v 2021 A] Training in extreme cold, his feet went numb at first, but his body adapted so that he became fine at 25°F {{kkbfHkyQ_q0@930}}
- [1v 2021 O] Bravado and arrogance can be a protection against demoralization {{389BOWoca-4@728}}
- [1v 2021 O] Whiny groups attract and convert others because blame culture is comfortable, and joining one is the worst thing you can do {{jciAEXnlO_w@253}}

- [1v 2021 O] Insecure people aren't virtuous; they lash out because they lack self-belief {{2ezr-xUf9v0@1381}}

## motivation / discipline / going / need (30)
- [2v 2020-2022 P] Motivation is fleeting, discipline is better, and the best state is not needing discipline because you love the training also: 2020-07: Fitness discipline is built over time through habits; you cannot rely on motivation, which is fleeting {{RPUhg61IV-g@0}} {{kKX0AUrXzIw@2199}}
- [2v 2020-2021 P] Motivation is fleeting and gets you going; discipline keeps you going. Build it by imitating disciplined people {{m1fD0HP6sIg@1760}} {{8ELQ2XZgpls@444}}
- [1v 2020 P] What matters most for a novice is building the discipline of coming in three times a week; motivation doesn't work in bodybuilding, routine does. {2020-02 {"frequency": "3x/week"}} {{_4wWgin1dVY@379}}
- [1v 2020 P] Going to the gym over-hyped isn't sustainable daily and going in depressed and low-energy yields no gains; find the middle ground, as with most of life, since extremes rarely pay off long term. {{j2u5TzD5fGg@569}}
- [1v 2020 P] Wanting to be big and strong is already the motivation you need; you don't need 'motivation DNA'. {{-i6A0etUfmE@378}}
- [1v 2020 P] Someone who needs insane discipline just to get average results builds a discipline that carries over to studies and work {{eeK8e2KIvwY@949}}
- [1v 2020 P] Once willpower is maxed out it becomes discipline: doing it whether it rains, it is minus 20 degrees or you are in pain; you transcend motivation {{eeK8e2KIvwY@1200}}
- [1v 2020 P] Training is discipline, not motivation: 5 to 8 pm is training time, no questions asked {2020-09 {"duration": "5-8 pm"}} {{Zg0oUngwPK4@459}}
- [1v 2020 O] Motivation to lift and bigorexia are closely related: motivation may simply be an expression of the dysmorphia, born from comparing yourself to others {{MuQvx5dNpno@89}}
- [1v 2021 P] There are three levels: motivation, discipline, and just doing it; long-time naturals train because it's on the schedule, without deliberation {{kkbfHkyQ_q0@825}}
- [1v 2021 P] Progression in the gym fuels the motivation to keep training {{Em00Z8i8V7c@0}}
- [1v 2021 P] Motivation is fire that ignites but burns out; discipline is cold and permanent; don't depend on motivational content to train {{8nUneZclRp0@1373}}
- [1v 2021 O] He never feels motivated, but he loves training; motivation is too unreliable to carry you {{0RoULYC_tao@63}}
- [1v 2021 P] Don't announce your discipline or practice; let it show through how you live {{KelEHl5w8Jk@1836}}
- [1v 2021 O] His three years of self-directed calisthenics forged his discipline; a mentor would have made him complacent {{aIjUTEtXOcM@815}}
- [1v 2021 P] Turn motivation into discipline and novelty into routine {{9mnLRQsrs9k@3268}}
- [1v 2022 P] Motivation is a short-lived high; build discipline through routine and habit so you still train when motivation is gone {{Bg7jQfbI1w8@255}}
- [1v 2022 P] Turn the resolution into dedication and motivation into discipline {{Bg7jQfbI1w8@758}}
- [1v 2022 P] Faith works like any discipline: a set of restrictions that must be reaffirmed daily or it withers, because the self keeps trying to revolt {{AJ8AhxJWas8@717}}
- [1v 2022 P] Motivation is the most important half, but you still need the method: without the pickaxe you break the rock with your forehead {{GowuEmDClqY@4152}}
- [1v 2022 P] Pick exercises you love so you can train with zero discipline {{kKX0AUrXzIw@2136}}
- [1v 2022 P] Relying on others for motivation is like a fire you must feed with outside wood; the motivation must come from inside you {{vdEfTs-aMmM@441}}
- [1v 2022 O] Most gains are achieved alone; if you need a crew or commercial gym to train, your motivation is fragile, as shown by people who stopped training during the pandemic lockdowns {{vdEfTs-aMmM@568}}
- [1v 2022 P] Motivation is an emotion that spikes and wears off; discipline is a flat, constant state that does not need emotional highs to get work done {{eWP2m4lLq_0@501}}
- [1v 2022 P] Once discipline becomes habit, training is no longer questioned and skipping stops being an option, like breathing {{eWP2m4lLq_0@690}}
- [1v 2023 P] When people mock you for lifting or improving, it proves your self-improvement bothers them and you should keep going {{Z2JQ5n0dbkY@3829}}
- [1v 2024 P] The masochistic approach to lifting must die; if you hate the gym you will not keep going, it should feel like a kid in a toy store {{qYaWxvA4fKg@2708}}
- [1v 2024 P] Teach discipline early: it gives kids a huge advantage, and it's much harder to build when you're older {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@10127}}
- [1v 2024 P] External motivation is never sustainable; motivation content fills people with empty motivation and keeps them from building discipline {{z9MttM0Bq-I@380}}
- [1v 2024 A] Gundill told that natural lifter he had small legs, lacked abs and chest and had bad calves; the lifter looked hurt, then turned the criticism into motivation, a lesson in standards {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@186}}

## talent / hard / smart / year (25)

- [1v 2021 O] Real combat experience, including defeat, is what reforms a talented fighter's mindset {{389BOWoca-4@931}}

- [1v 2023 P] Having talent is not enough; without will, strength is nothing {{Ux-tXsicqIk@1827}}

## result / give / wonder / journey (20)
- [1v 2020 O] Many natural lifters give up and then wonder why they don't get results. {{Pa9RvC5Es0U@377}}

- [1v 2021 P] If something gives you progress and results, keep doing it even if science or he disagrees; listen to your body {{_L5wLHlFfI0@125}}
- [1v 2021 P] His favourite quote: it's a dishonour for the soul to give up while the body still holds out {{xGoJNq78htU@0}}
- [1v 2021 P] Over-focusing on diet gives subpar results; training is the most important thing {{XASWAdsSu0M@1207}}
- [1v 2021 P] Wanting results without paying the price makes you insatiable and silly; effort and time cap how much anyone can accumulate {{TD_WzcWEmFg@625}}
- [1v 2021 P] Sasaki Kojiro, beaten many times but never stopping, is the patron saint of people who must work twice as hard for results; aim for slow incremental progress {{pDfGvxia37A@1014}}
- [1v 2021 O] Showing your results gives others permission and proof that they can do it too {{yr4zj-WbG4I@994}}
- [1v 2021 P] 'No pain, no gain, no brain, no gain': suffering without intelligence gives no results {{qcRCMVABEQM@1052}}
- [1v 2022 P] Over-focusing on results makes people ignore the journey and never reach the results; the results are the by-product of doing it for the difficulty {{ZPBwBrOktgA@192}}
- [1v 2022 P] No method can guarantee results; embracing uncertainty and experimentation is the whole journey {{g9YniHngWDI@1600}}
- [1v 2023 P] When you're obsessed with a muscle group, give in to it; your best gains come where the passion is {{43F9unmSc_I@124}}
- [1v 2024 P] Focus on the journey, not the result; result-oriented lifters tend to quit {{lGbT0aT2hOk@1894}}
- [1v 2024 O] The ancient Greeks never separated mind and body (Plato means wide shoulders; philosophers wrestled for hours); seeing bodybuilding as spiritual keeps you going when results slow {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@3598}}
- [1v 2024 O] Keep your phone out of training: put music on shuffle and do not touch it; people scrolling between sets rush their work and then wonder why they do not grow {{6p2x4kDDjzs@630}}

- [1v 2022 O] Agrees with Eubank that the best program is one you can stick to and that you should do movements you enjoy {{0pKnDucWcDc@1572}}
- [1v 2024 P] If you lose your love for lifting, try a new sport and do only the movements you enjoy {{okm6bZHKHI4@8125}}
- [1v 2024 P] If you train and eat things you don't enjoy you are hurting your gains; pick a split and lifts you enjoy, the idea that bodybuilding must suck is pushed by PED users {{D5AUHq98RdY@10513}}

## day / grind / every / learn (18)
- [1v 2020 P] The lifter who grinds every rep often drops out from injury; bide your time, stay injury-free and put the work in. {{QiOh8SF-UxQ@427}}
- [1v 2020 O] He rejects the idea that training should be a grind you sometimes hate; not every day is great, but you should work to make every day pleasurable so you keep going. {{TwuT80wJql8@438}}
- [1v 2020 P] Lift in a neutral, determined but calm state; PRs set in that state are easy to replicate day to day, while tension and stress can make you miss lifts. {{j2u5TzD5fGg@380}}
- [1v 2020 P] Like Netero says, a man who stops challenging himself is already dead; you are never done learning and there is no perfection {{2PyZ82unsj8@187}}

- [1v 2020 P] Cheat meals create psychological dependence and make you hate your diet while waiting for the one day you eat what you like; eat things you like every day instead. {{2sIS1fZYqrc@191}}
- [1v 2020 P] The way means staying humble and learning every day; stop learning and you stagnate {{Wz3bs32Qo68@1067}}
- [1v 2020 P] Being open to other ideas completes you, but flip-flopping to every new idea is walking in circles {{J99Xzu8JbqY@632}}
- [1v 2020 P] Discipline is rented, not owned, and rent is due every day {{39mSVxrifu8@379}}

- [1v 2021 P] Take moments every day without phone, book or TV to simply think; if you cannot sit still like that, it signals anxiety that needs fixing {{jciAEXnlO_w@1513}}
- [1v 2021 O] Lifters who just grind through training they hate don't last long {{0RoULYC_tao@811}}
- [1v 2021 P] You lift to get better at your sport; the day you lift just to lift, you stop being an athlete {{cNGPCn9Uupc@1635}}
- [1v 2023 O] Eating the same food every day reinforces his neurotic traits, so he keeps both diet and training flexible; rigid structure breaks badly {{TxDUQPe3B0E@1323}}
- [1v 2023 P] The day a man thinks he is done learning is the day he dies {{DAZRYoex8_w@1647}}
- [1v 2024 P] Real self-improvement builds on who you are; renouncing yourself entirely is denial, and grind gurus whose whole personality is the grind are empty {{RPW-PxhYqrA@2201}}
- [1v 2024 P] What you need is to stick with it, learn and keep grinding; PEDs hide laziness, ignorance and a fragile ego {{Q5ECkNZYZ58@692}}

## gym / hour / spend / long (17)
- [1v 2020 O] He spends long hours in the gym partly because he loves being there. {{cHYOYTgiRlM@250}}

- [1v 2020 O] The gym is not social time; chatting and phone use add time that is not training {{7e0gcEEa-ms@0}}

- [1v 2020 O] Being a serious lifter starts at around 8-10 hours a week in the gym, enough that training replaces hobbies or social time {2020-10 {"hours": "8-10/wk"}} {{kh--k3genZA@755}}
- [1v 2021 P] As long as you train with adequate severity and give your body time to recover, every workout is the purest form of love for it, and it repays you by growing bigger, stronger and more resilient {{TPPCu5zBPLk@270}}
- [1v 2021 P] Visualisation is not magic: thinking about your goal keeps you motivated and consistent, and people obsessed with the gym for long periods get the best physiques {{PYICG0hBlVo@1700}}

- [1v 2022 P] Cap gaming at roughly an hour a day and maybe two hours on weekend days, and never spend more hours per week gaming than training {2022-04 {"time": "1 h/day, 2 h weekend"}} {{bM23N1IPrDk@7059}}
- [1v 2023 P] In the gym you are your own opponent; adversity and a fire to train harder are what most recreational lifters lack {{rFfLUloDHeg@844}}
- [1v 2023 P] Anything done in the gym is functional as long as it aligns with your goal {{ZN5ohmPPfjM@881}}
- [1v 2024 P] Constant PRs keep him in love with the gym {{l6L6HlGM4F0@752}}
- [1v 2024 O] Training about 5 hours a week needs no lifestyle changes, it replaces Netflix and Instagram time, but do not center your whole life around the gym; that will not make you happy {2024-09 {"frequency": "5 h/wk"}} {{bRKQIOYo2u4@843}}
- [1v 2024 A] Social anxiety is a phobia treated by gradual exposure; in 2019 three people in a house made him panic, now he can spend 5 hours with 20 strangers; it took about three years {2024-09 {"years": 3}} {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@5368}}

- [1v 2020 P] Training has a massive influence on mental health {{ewvy5V03gTE@0}}
- [1v 2020 A] NH says he, like many lifters, suffers from muscle dysmorphia (bigorexia), summed up as the persistent feeling 'I am too small' {{MuQvx5dNpno@34}}
- [1v 2020 O] Bigorexia is more dangerous than other dysmorphias because its fulfilment seems doable, which keeps you chasing it {{MuQvx5dNpno@120}}
- [1v 2020 P] Dysmorphia turns against you when progress stalls because muscle can only grow so fast; motivation is a fiery form of anxiety {{MuQvx5dNpno@308}}

- [1v 2022 A] A psychiatrist told him poor mental health is marked by failing to align the physical and the spiritual {{GeMs1MAEK60@2253}}
- [1v 2022 P] Comparing your unpumped mirror reflection to curated pictures is bad for mental health {{eCR3dcmCPV8@188}}
- [1v 2024 P] Body dysmorphia drives overly aggressive methods that stall progress, which deepens the dysmorphia: a downward spiral {{yX1br2PHa0w@957}}
- [1v 2024 O] Nobody can make you train; find the reason in yourself, because training improves mental health, longevity and strength {{okm6bZHKHI4@3184}}
- [1v 2024 O] Only feeling comfortable when very lean is body dysmorphia, often fuelled by social media {{iKSAOABWj-c@720}}
- [1v 2024 P] Loneliness kills young men; seek community (church, mosque, campus) and prioritise mental health if isolation will last years {{o40xCjIzl14@6064}}

## willpower / internal / external / locu (16)
- [1v 2020 P] Internal willpower is unseen discipline, like training when you do not want to or skipping bad food; external willpower is shown to others, like standing your ground {{KTYf4E4YcB8@509}}
- [1v 2020 O] People who loudly display willpower, like announcing a diet to 50 people, often lack internal willpower {{KTYf4E4YcB8@600}}
- [1v 2020 O] Only internal willpower makes you a doormat; only external makes you a tyrant; a balance is needed {{KTYf4E4YcB8@572}}
- [1v 2020 O] Unused willpower rusts; you must practice it {{KTYf4E4YcB8@634}}
- [1v 2020 P] The only way to build discipline is to train; willpower grows only when put into action {{39mSVxrifu8@315}}
- [1v 2021 P] Bodybuilding needs willpower plus the curiosity to try new methods and analyse the results {{6K7xyjI5lYE@189}}
- [1v 2022 O] Lifting is a good path toward the Übermensch because it is an external, repeated action that builds habits and facilitates internal work, though it is not enough alone {{WdFEEkLfBRc@3155}}
- [1v 2022 O] Masculinity is mostly internal; muscle and other outward signs are only the visible tip of the iceberg, and modern men over-focus on appearance {{WdFEEkLfBRc@250}}
- [1v 2022 P] Locus of control is a learned belief, not innate, so you can switch from external to internal {{vdEfTs-aMmM@1009}}
- [1v 2022 P] Discipline is built through external actions you fully own; do not depend on idols or stimulants to get things done {{eWP2m4lLq_0@3960}}

- [1v 2023 P] Fitness is fixing yourself, which requires telling people what's wrong; an external locus of control is antithetical to self-improvement {{kXdKs8Fu2WU@881}}
- [1v 2024 P] Willpower is not constant; it fluctuates with mood and hormones, and anyone claiming otherwise is hiding vulnerability {{qYaWxvA4fKg@3531}}
- [1v 2024 A] At 16-17 he could only get up at 6 a.m. to run by playing a motivational clip; his mother asked whether he was a puppet without willpower, which made him see his dependence {{z9MttM0Bq-I@697}}
- [1v 2025 P] The core problem behind most miserable lives, including incels, is willpower: they deny the one factor that would get them out of the hole {{kptuCb6_2Cc@150}}
- [1v 2025 P] Healthy adults understand the internal world drives decisions; incels let the external world justify everything {{kptuCb6_2Cc@2463}}

## pleasure / bad / painful / chas (16)
- [1v 2020 O] People who avoid hard work like high reps because they're painful won't amount to anything anyway; his advice is for people who push themselves. {{Eh0fnXIyDMc@0}}

- [1v 2021 P] People who always chase pleasure lack mental preparation, complain constantly and lack resilience; resisting pleasure is itself a form of resilience {{Da6d5cecRKA@986}}

- [1v 2021 A] A single bad friend can derail an otherwise good person's life {{7ddQIjZoujQ@1406}}
- [1v 2021 A] You mimic the company you keep; he has seen good people turn bad after hanging out with bad people {{7ddQIjZoujQ@816}}

- [1v 2023 P] Just because a path is painful and difficult does not make it the right path; men tend to self-destruct for its own sake and must unlearn this {{SS5uAU1rcEo@4204}}

## pain / regret / worth / discipline (14)
- [2v 2021-2024 P] It is better to have remorse than regrets: act and maybe regret the act rather than never act, because regret is inaction and inaction is stagnation also: 2024-09: It is better to live with remorse than with regret: acting and failing beats inaction, because you can apologize and grow from a mistake {{ilGY8qFjuxI@1592}} {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@526}}

- [1v 2021 P] The pain barrier stops people from lifting, but pushing past it is worth it 100 times over {{eKBDacpRYSU@2514}}
- [1v 2021 P] Pain is just a sensation; lifting teaches that everything worth doing is hard {{yr4zj-WbG4I@2224}}

- [1v 2022 P] Change requires pain, and the pain of the pursuit is nothing next to the regret of never trying {{U_4FtEW5nh0@191}}

- [1v 2022 P] Discipline is pain you accept to avoid a greater pain: regret, not achieving anything, lying to yourself {{AJ8AhxJWas8@854}}

- [1v 2024 P] Hold yourself to higher standards than you hold others {{jmOPC_ozpwc@6599}}

- [1v 2022 P] Being stubborn with one method that has proven not to work is like backing up to run harder into a wall {{E1er3oQ3IeM@1440}}

- [1v 2020 A] Doing the thing kills anxiety: skipping deadlifts built anxiety until he went back and it vanished {{Qgpc7evE7_4@1011}}
- [1v 2021 P] After a lapse, assess it but do not pass moral judgment on yourself; self-flagellation feeds the cycle of repetition {{Uzm8hsjuYcY@770}}

- [1v 2022 P] Diligent repetition turns into habit and reveals itself as discipline {{U_4FtEW5nh0@191}}

- [1v 2022 P] Tactic 1, chaining: pair training with a habit you already have, e.g. lift before your 2 pm meal, so the habit chain is harder to break {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@1216}}
- [1v 2022 P] Will is a finite reserve that grows with use; conserve it by building habits so you can draw on it when habits fail {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@1164}}
- [1v 2022 P] Stop chasing novelty; the best bodies were built through consistency and you should stay on a program while it works {{wB-ydKSfQ5k@2146}}
- [1v 2022 P] Greatness is built from tiny daily habits, like a temple built one rock a day, rather than from occasional hyped-up bursts {{eWP2m4lLq_0@627}}
- [1v 2024 P] Happiness is an act and contentment its result; you must keep up the habits that built it {{Dciz-VeNHVI@3344}}
- [1v 2024 P] Change is fractional and slow; old habits and self-limiting beliefs return if you stop watching them, so self-improvement is trading bad habits and beliefs for good ones {{AZSZmhOJZJI@1079}}
- [1v 2024 P] A good habit-building video should essentially tell you to turn it off and go do the thing; excellence is built by doing it always {{z9MttM0Bq-I@1197}}
- [1v 2024 P] The mindset built by simply training is what got experienced lifters where they are; refinement and optimization come after {{RPc1lnNNXNg@1330}}

## far / defeat / victory / teach (14)
- [1v 2020 O] Most people skip isolation work at the end of the workout far more often than compounds, telling themselves the heavy stuff was enough; that is the wrong mindset. {{7Ax5O5DKoUg@436}}
- [1v 2020 P] In a fight and in training you are really trying to defeat yourself, not the opponent {{O-x2v688wrg@628}}
- [1v 2020 P] Don't benchmark your progress against a YouTuber; you may have far more strength potential {{-cPcWQqMRC8@1294}}
- [1v 2021 O] There are far more mental slaves than physical ones, possibly billions, and they put the chains on themselves and can remove them {{lsMyNBeNPvk@400}}
- [1v 2022 P] Training like a normal person for two years is far more work and far more effective than training like a madman for two weeks; change must be incremental {{he2KJeMcoAI@1830}}
- [1v 2022 P] Consistency beats intensity: training steadily for a few years is far better than going super hard for two weeks and quitting {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@354}}
- [1v 2022 P] Environment shapes a person, but how he interacts with it decides whether he thrives; Guts had a far harder life and did not become a doomer {{nZnt4--nIpY@194}}
- [1v 2022 O] Weight on the bar is far more gratifying than slow measurement changes, which explains the popularity of 5x5 and Starting Strength-style templates and why many people chasing size drift into strength {{LxGdGChvk2c@3387}}
- [1v 2022 P] Defeat teaches far more than victory {{SDVF9vWBQAM@252}}
- [1v 2023 O] Optimists who live in constant positive feedback shatter when they fail, while active pessimists absorb defeat and keep going {{Ux-tXsicqIk@3847}}
- [1v 2024 P] Discipline taken too far becomes a straitjacket: rigid schedules that stop you enjoying life {{lGbT0aT2hOk@825}}
- [1v 2024 P] Learning how to lose is a more valuable skill than winning, because you will lose far more than you win and the cycle never ends {{AZSZmhOJZJI@4180}}
- [1v 2024 P] Failures teach far more than successes; a victory just confirms your game plan, while a loss gives you homework. This applies to the gym too, and seeing failure as an opportunity removes it as an enemy {{Plr3f3LXO_o@1014}}
- [1v 2025 P] Being surrounded by people better than you raises your level far more than being the best among weak peers {{0O0nG2jLadU@8435}}

- [1v 2020 P] How long results take doesn't matter; it isn't a race, you'll train for the rest of your life {{vC9cKh9tFxs@930}}

- [1v 2020 P] Training for one, 10 or 100 days isn't discipline, however hard; discipline is resilience beyond time and intensity {{39mSVxrifu8@188}}
- [1v 2020 P] Comparing yourself to gifted people makes your genetics look bad, but life isn't a race against everyone {{xCzoFKSPkIc@312}}
- [1v 2020 O] Like muscularity, intelligence keeps improving with time; if you think you were as smart 10 years ago, you stopped progressing {2020-12 {"years": 10}} {{Lg-w_lgCgUc@1316}}
- [1v 2021 P] Results justify effort, but results take time; comparing yourself to someone 15 times your size with 10 years of training kills drive, so only your own progression matters {{j1bS37-7AHA@1560}}
- [1v 2021 P] Do not look for difficulty, look for greatness; the path to greatness is hard anyway, and people who chase complexity lose sight of what matters {{Da6d5cecRKA@2736}}
- [1v 2021 P] Building muscle isn't hard, it takes a long time; mixing up long and hard makes people quit {{74XVh8ghvs4@2323}}
- [1v 2022 O] Long, repetitive programs mostly create mental fatigue; people get mediocre results for two months and quit {2022-02 {"weeks": 8}} {{wLx5BRx8AaU@1866}}

## choose / love / cannot / stick (13)
- [1v 2021 P] Office workers can specialise more freely than soldiers, who need to run and climb, but should keep a fun side pursuit so they are not trapped if their love for one practice dies {{kCVudEfVU0c@438}}
- [1v 2021 P] You cannot stop pain signals but you can choose to ignore them; many people refuse to admit their own power because admitting it makes every mishap their responsibility {{jciAEXnlO_w@191}}
- [1v 2021 P] Select a path and stick to it regardless of what society says is worth pursuing {{Rf00mkH4QkQ@318}}
- [1v 2021 P] The proper response to provocation is pride, not cowardice; always entertain the option of fighting, which then lets you choose whether to engage {{Rf00mkH4QkQ@63}}
- [1v 2022 P] You cannot help chronic self-pitiers with attention; it teaches them helplessness earns validation, and only self-administered tough love works {{nZnt4--nIpY@1146}}
- [1v 2022 P] Choose a form of training you love; you cannot stick with something you hate {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@2230}}
- [1v 2022 P] The real punishment is inaction; you choose to stay in the environment, so leave blackpill forums and stop consuming the poison {{EdfnJor78OQ@2902}}
- [1v 2022 P] You cannot talk yourself into being proud or love a body you dislike; pride comes only from building something worth loving {{eCR3dcmCPV8@936}}
- [1v 2023 P] Find the triggers behind anger outbursts and where they come from; you cannot kill your demons with hate, only with love {{XQEmQq-Ga2g@2401}}
- [1v 2024 P] You can love your body and still want more; using self-hatred as fuel works at first and then destroys you, while love and acceptance kept him going {{qYaWxvA4fKg@1644}}
- [1v 2024 P] Self-improvement is a climb where you cannot stay still: you go up or you go down, and the direction you choose matters more than winning or losing {{AZSZmhOJZJI@1207}}
- [1v 2024 P] No one is made for something; you choose a pursuit, stick to it, and that makes you made for it {{AZSZmhOJZJI@4810}}
- [1v 2024 O] A woman who makes you choose between her and the gym is not worth it unless she is a gym-cell {{jmOPC_ozpwc@1988}}

## beat / consistency / trump / park (11)
- [2v 2021-2022 P] The wrong thing done consistently beats the right thing done sparingly: a kid doing daily chin-ups and push-ups at the park beats someone running a perfect paid program every two weeks also: 2021-03: Consistency trumps optimisation every time: a guy lifting rocks and doing chin-ups daily in a park beats someone doing a perfect workout once a week {{_L5wLHlFfI0@125}} {{he2KJeMcoAI@2144}}
- [1v 2020 P] Being consistent for a full year beats being obsessed for two weeks {{RPUhg61IV-g@190}}
- [1v 2020 P] Mindset and effort matter more than the program, especially for novices: a park trainee who goes hard on chin-ups and push-ups will outgain someone with a perfect program who doesn't put in the work {{o9uDgF5J89U@64}}
- [1v 2021 O] Obsession can trump optimisation: training a body part relentlessly even with poor methods can outdo someone doing it properly {{aIjUTEtXOcM@566}}
- [1v 2021 A] A kid NH mocked at nine kept training for years and beat him in that exam {{n-n3txKjGmE@943}}
- [1v 2022 P] Consistency beats both hard work and smart work; the guy who shows up every session wins, and every peak physique is built on it {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@578}}
- [1v 2023 O] Enjoyment of a lift matters more than its hypertrophy rating because adherence drives consistency; a C-tier lift you love beats an S-tier lift you don't {{CGhPZ9AxBJE@1134}}
- [1v 2023 P] Consistency trumps random high-burn intensity {{JSOvpTDcSvA@2451}}
- [1v 2023 P] Sustained effort over years beats short intense bursts {{16fjHEtZTCA@1839}}
- [1v 2024 P] Consistency beats theory: a guy doing park push-ups and pull-ups with a rock backpack will outdo a theory-perfect lifter who can't stick to it {{jDk3LytTEjA@2778}}
- [1v 2024 P] Someone enjoying a random program will beat someone hating a perfect one, because consistency drives results in everything {{6yW3GSskl8g@1709}}

## sets / listen / music / during (10)
- [2v 2020 P] He listens to educational talks during warm-ups and volume work and keeps music only for hard sets, because that hypes him up more also: 2020-12: Listen to podcasts or something educational during warm-ups and keep the music for working sets so it stays special {{m1fD0HP6sIg@566}} {{CojMme7jr3o@311}}
- [2v 2024 O] Constantly listening to music is a sign of overstimulation and avoiding your own thoughts also: 2024-09: Listening to music all day is an addiction and a sign you can't stand your own thoughts {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@9017}} {{fPU3nRIH8sM@9411}}
- [1v 2020 A] He never wants to fail; he hits his strength PR knowing he could have pushed more, then enters the volume phase 'like a winner' and sets volume PRs. {{QiOh8SF-UxQ@128}}
- [1v 2020 P] Warm-ups often feel awkward (e.g. 275 lb when top sets are 390), so do not let them psych you out; they are not an indicator of how the work sets will go {2020-06 {"load": "275 lb vs 390 lb top"}} {{4rzc99lfDOA@63}}
- [1v 2020 O] Because we judge advice by strength, gifted lifters stop listening to the 99% of lifters weaker than them {{-1dECrWYeFk@572}}
- [1v 2021 O] Doing repeated favors for someone makes you like them; similarly an audience that keeps listening to a creator concludes he must be superior {{j1bS37-7AHA@1780}}
- [1v 2021 P] Don't listen to music for the whole session, because you blunt its adrenaline boost; use podcasts or language lessons for warm-ups and save hard music for working sets {{QTHtVBP_36g@2618}}
- [1v 2023 P] Rehearse your sets mentally during the day (e.g. four sets of overhead press planned for the evening); it builds belief and frees focus during the real sets {2023-02 {"sets": 4}} {{UWr5TQVT63g@1569}}
- [1v 2023 O] Goofiness, such as dancing between sets, is anabolic {{B5eAFxfON3A@505}}
- [1v 2024 A] He didn't skip a single gym day during the crisis; bodybuilding kept him together mentally {{sZntx8mqaH8@1510}}

## big / legs / arms / build (12)
- [1v 2020 O] Your bodybuilding goals are your own: if you want big legs or big arms, build them. {{4IhOKe7lo_s@316}}
- [1v 2020 O] Attraction should eventually stop being your number one goal; men who never move past it build an aesthetic physique (abs, pecs, arms) and often skip legs and upper back. {{U7WtpNeiRjI@63}}
- [1v 2020 P] Calves are always programmed last, so skipping the end of sessions gives chicken legs; finishing them is where you build mental fortitude {{cGzlMRCxvxo@1340}}
- [1v 2020 P] If you keep training and eating, you will get big; repeat that mantra daily {{WyxeMHovPJ8@920}}
- [1v 2021 O] Repeated smaller adversities build you up; a man who never met difficulty crumbles at the first big wave {{4NzW3-pvuGM@64}}
- [1v 2021 O] Training should be so central to your life that you would sooner lose your nails than stop; if you do not like training you will never get big {{YHm-qSHgGSk@946}}
- [1v 2021 O] Lifting is the best immunization against forced collectivism because it gives direct feedback from your own work; strong, big naturals tend to have a strong, big self {{K6R91uSnsPY@1933}}
- [1v 2021 P] Stop thinking about inflated standards and just train; 1940s lifters didn't debate whether they'd get 19-inch arms, they did the work {2021-11 {"arm": "19 in"}} {{Tos7dHreF3k@1197}}
- [1v 2022 P] Be grateful: having two arms, two legs and enough food to grow leaves you no excuse not to try your best {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@1947}}
- [1v 2022 P] Chase two rabbits and you catch neither: a boxer never tries to get as big as possible because it would hurt his boxing {{kAVZsNLPNQ4@2647}}
- [1v 2023 P] Training matters more than diet for getting big: you can't out-train a bad diet when cutting, but no amount of food builds muscle without gym work {{TxDUQPe3B0E@1451}}
- [1v 2024 O] Leg-day elitism is silly; training legs heavy is a preference, and someone who only wants an upper body is entitled to it {{Eta-sxecx9E@700}}

## help / gett / stronger / professional (12)
- [1v 2020 P] An addictive personality means getting hooked easily, which is dangerous because moderation matters and toxic substances degrade body or mind. {{UXzapA1Q6Mk@0}}
- [1v 2020 P] PRs aren't essential for bodybuilding, but knowing you're getting stronger helps mentally. {{2QnD9k0SbwA@252}}
- [1v 2020 P] Getting professional help or medication for body dysmorphia is fine if you need it {{MuQvx5dNpno@770}}
- [1v 2020 P] Sustainability and frequency are key: there is no point getting jacked if it lasts two weeks {{WyxeMHovPJ8@878}}
- [1v 2021 O] Most of gym culture is people pushing others down because they feel bad about themselves; confident people help others {{mW8_jnUR8gg@1100}}
- [1v 2022 O] Getting bigger makes you mentally stronger and more confident because you are harder to dominate {{eC9Srr3ZygE@1672}}
- [1v 2022 P] Everyone should spar with a professional boxer once to learn how little they actually know {{rVy03_C5jEU@1258}}
- [1v 2022 O] Getting implants signals an unresolved complex or body dysmorphia; surgeons profit from the spiral {{crP2w97XXfs@1572}}
- [1v 2022 P] A minimalist refuses to do more even knowing it helps; a maximalist refuses to do less even knowing it helps; do what works and is convenient {{Zv9QAIb5nGk@2773}}
- [1v 2023 P] Facing a stronger man, the only real fix for inferiority is to become stronger, not to convince yourself he is the idiot {{pBszDfFvoVI@1695}}
- [1v 2023 P] Refusing all help out of self-sacrifice is a virtue turned pathological; keep asking for help even after being rejected {{SS5uAU1rcEo@3628}}
- [1v 2024 P] Self-sacrifice is noble, but helping others only works if you stay self-sustainable; constantly spreading yourself thin turns kind people bitter {{wBct3Pl8OY4@1977}}

## sin / pride / becom / envy (12)
- [1v 2020 P] Lust becomes a fitness sin when chasing sex or power distracts you from becoming the best you can be. {{U7WtpNeiRjI@312}}
- [1v 2020 P] Confidence, self-assurance and even telling yourself you are the best are good when they keep you motivated; pride becomes a sin when used to attack others or make them feel worse {{2PyZ82unsj8@62}}
- [1v 2020 P] Envy becomes a sin when it turns into jealousy and resentment toward the people who have what you want {{zt2-wI7GJ-c@125}}
- [1v 2020 P] Bodybuilders who make it often forget where they came from; remembering your weak past makes you a relatable teacher and keeps you motivated {{cTHJUS0maL4@813}}
- [1v 2020 P] Include patriotic music from your own country because pride is motivating {{CojMme7jr3o@621}}
- [1v 2021 P] Envying high-status men makes you resent what you most desire and ultimately yourself; hero worship turning to resentment is common and dangerous in teenagers {{p42qv-VEEOk@1385}}
- [1v 2021 P] Admiration makes you want to copy someone and pushes you to act; envy is resentment from believing you will never have what they have, and it keeps you from working on it {{Da6d5cecRKA@103}}
- [1v 2022 P] Hard-earned body development breeds pride and confidence, traits essential and attractive in men {{GeMs1MAEK60@375}}
- [1v 2022 P] Pride that comes from identity alone, especially unearned identity, turns into arrogance; real pride is built through action {{j72vYLtzBxw@1446}}
- [1v 2022 O] Spreading genetics talk to others creates a crabs-in-a-bucket culture where anyone with ambition gets attacked; doing this to someone is your worst sin {{EdfnJor78OQ@2209}}
- [1v 2022 P] Make the first task easy to build momentum, and include leisure items in the list so you always keep moving {{u8IRHcUWfPs@755}}
- [1v 2024 O] Victims are partly responsible because they let abuse happen; just as a country without laws becomes hell, you must build your own police force of boundaries {{v1URYz8VF5E@1505}}

## days / schedul / lazines / day (12)
- [1v 2020 P] Sloth is the unwillingness to do something you know you should do, not because you can't but because you don't feel like it; even passionate lifters have days without the fire. {{tQvMIw1PjjY@63}}
- [1v 2020 P] Scheduled days off make you eager to return, while impromptu skipped days breed more laziness {{Qgpc7evE7_4@884}}
- [1v 2020 O] On upper days mental stamina limits you; on lower days physical stamina runs out first {{cGzlMRCxvxo@1804}}
- [1v 2021 O] The days you don't want to train are the days you should; within about 40 minutes the reluctance is gone, and he has never regretted going to the gym {2021-05 {"time": "40 min"}} {{8nUneZclRp0@1685}}
- [1v 2021 P] If you must miss a scheduled session, push it back and rearrange the program rather than drop it; skipping teaches you laziness {{8nUneZclRp0@1250}}
- [1v 2021 P] Scheduled rest days are not skipped days; missing a scheduled training day means you were under-recovered from poor programming or your training is not fun {{0RoULYC_tao@0}}
- [1v 2022 P] Laziness breeds laziness and motion builds motion; desk workers who sit all day have the most reason to train after work {{3W-yE2pgTec@1197}}
- [1v 2022 O] Training is an escape from your day job (he programs 10 hours a day at a desk), so by definition it isn't functional to your daily motions {2022-06 {"hours": 10}} {{kAVZsNLPNQ4@2390}}
- [1v 2022 P] Write schedules on paper rather than on a phone; a material copy commits you psychologically. In grad school he survived with daily paper schedules plus a weekly sheet {{u8IRHcUWfPs@439}}
- [1v 2023 O] Leg days reveal whether someone is a serious lifter; many people skip or half-ass them {{6KA80BQ9W3k@0}}
- [1v 2024 O] Self-discipline is one of humanity's earliest moral attainments and a reason we became the apex species; a cat given 15 days of food eats it in 3 {2024-11 {"days": "15 vs 3"}} {{fPU3nRIH8sM@2251}}
- [1v 2024 P] Accept bad days: a day where nothing happens is the normal standard, happiness is a positive deviation; on hormonal 'blue days' just live through it and you feel better the next day {{TvKzeUKgZcY@319}}

## limit / impossible / brain / factor (12)
- [1v 2020 O] Letting an old man's comment keep you out of the gym for a month shows a weak mind; do not take advice from people who believe in a natural limit {{7XqE7GhDsgc@64}}
- [1v 2020 P] The point of 'nothing is impossible' is to remove the limits in your mind; if real limits exist nature will remind you {{JXVJtNrIZ9U@822}}
- [1v 2020 P] Comparing to others hurts both ways: a strong lifter with small shoulders may wrongly think he's at his limit despite strength to drive growth; a weaker one may give up {{-cPcWQqMRC8@1700}}
- [1v 2020 P] Obstacles feed on fear you created; once you accept a goal as impossible it becomes impossible {{uL-pwl2nfa0@1577}}
- [1v 2020 A] Limiting factors are almost always mental; when he stopped telling himself what his body could do and let it show him, it was far more capable than he thought {{KRhP2ZLI3C0@1370}}
- [1v 2021 P] Never call something impossible; ask under what conditions it is possible. The only person who can limit you is yourself {{EFhzEtjSG6s@195}}
- [1v 2021 P] The real limiting factor in muscle growth is your brain, i.e. knowledge, not your body {{8ELQ2XZgpls@814}}
- [1v 2024 P] Natural plateaus are usually intellectual limits, not physical ones; the brain stops finding ways to grow {{vVQmYGw7Kj8@1765}}
- [1v 2024 A] His knowledge comes from reading everything with curiosity, connecting it all into a network; social media eats the limited brain energy that this work requires {{D5AUHq98RdY@3995}}
- [1v 2024 P] People do not attempt goals mostly from fear of how failure would feel; saying it is impossible really means I am not enough {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@817}}
- [1v 2024 O] Records that stood for 10-30 years get broken repeatedly once one person breaks them, showing belief was the limit {2024-12 {"years": "10-30"}} {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@754}}
- [1v 2025 P] A methodology should structure your training, not limit it; he wants to use free weights, machines and cables alike {{ACFKXT44BzM@1506}}

## six / month / year / after (12)
- [1v 2020 P] If after six months you keep skipping part of your program, ask whether it's laziness (then move it earlier to force yourself) or because you instinctively know it's not needed (then remove it, since it only creates guilt and mental stress). {2020-04 {"months": 6}} {{fSOiGuUPqBc@1006}}
- [1v 2020 P] First get stable, around a six out of ten every day, then focus on becoming a better bodybuilder {{ewvy5V03gTE@694}}
- [1v 2020 P] Detach emotionally from main-lift numbers; a pure squat stalling for three months is fine if other knee-flexion lifts progress {2020-09 {"weeks": 12}} {{9M7QVlvFdQY@200}}
- [1v 2020 P] Inability to project years ahead feeds the natural-limit idea; over six weeks little changes, over three to five years you look different {2020-09 {"years": "3-5"}} {{-cPcWQqMRC8@1858}}
- [1v 2021 P] The person who trains properly for 10 years outgrows the one who trains very intensely for six months then quits {2021-03 {"years": 10}} {{kkbfHkyQ_q0@805}}
- [1v 2021 P] Someone who trains like a madman for six months makes less progress than someone who trains fairly hard for two years {2021-05 {"months": 6, "years": 2}} {{0RoULYC_tao@375}}
- [1v 2021 P] Patience is the key virtue: do not train intensely for two weeks then quit; stick to it and hard work tells no lies {{VzhzaWFG6bE@436}}
- [1v 2021 P] Never set a hard deadline for a goal body; promises like 'a god physique in six months' are marketing, and a good physique doesn't come in six months without drugs {2021-09 {"weeks": "6 months"}} {{PYICG0hBlVo@946}}
- [1v 2021 P] Choose your destination and regularly ask why you are training; months of training without a purpose lead to disappointment and wasted energy {{I2aR7F5rCuE@1672}}
- [1v 2022 P] The cycle of discipline, pain, confidence and gratitude never ends; lifters who quit after six months lose all the benefits {2022-02 {"weeks": 26}} {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@2212}}
- [1v 2022 P] One day, after six months or six years, you wake up and love yourself; you cannot predict when {{JNBgBfmTqx8@1636}}
- [1v 2024 P] After six months porn-free the hardest part is done; just stay away from it {2024-11 {"months": 6}} {{fPU3nRIH8sM@7873}}

## step / first / dictate / gett (12)
- [1v 2020 P] Natural bodybuilding is walking a thousand-mile road step by step; trying to make huge headway each session, like training twice a week and killing yourself, leads to injury and no progress {{WTssQqG_a24@886}}
- [1v 2020 P] Stop viewing life in weekly or monthly blocks; focus on getting through one day, since the first step makes the mountain surmountable (blank-page stress) {{KRhP2ZLI3C0@920}}
- [1v 2020 O] Purity is going straight from A to B; corruption creeps in through extra steps, so pursue goals with single-minded focus {{Lg-w_lgCgUc@1444}}
- [1v 2021 P] First step: feel the anger but don't let it dictate action; the enlightened step is to not feel the insult at all {{fsyx5y-VYns@250}}
- [1v 2021 P] Apply stoic virtues step by step, not all at once {{xGoJNq78htU@502}}
- [1v 2021 P] Self-love is the first step to the happy life; self-hatred locks the path {{pqJjfS-3XDk@2314}}
- [1v 2022 P] Change has three steps: wish it, will it, then get it done, and getting it done requires discipline {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@390}}
- [1v 2022 A] He enjoys lifting but does not let hedonism dictate training length; he does what is necessary and goes home {{PuIp9cGKMHo@540}}
- [1v 2022 P] You are both the zombie and the warrior; choose struggle as your religion, and the hardest part, the first step, is behind you {{JvTZaoHqLts@258}}
- [1v 2022 P] Accepting your inferiority is the first step to becoming superior; to be good at bodybuilding you must first suck at it {{_KT_9vmHNzE@3152}}
- [1v 2023 P] Self-awareness about your demons is the first step to change; vulnerable narcissists never admit it {{XQEmQq-Ga2g@2463}}
- [1v 2024 P] If training robs you of connections or other passions, step back: using lifting as escapism defines the gymcel {{lGbT0aT2hOk@1948}}

## bully / physical / fight / easier (12)
- [1v 2020 P] Being a bodybuilder is more than lifting weights; the mental aspect is as important as the physical and they can't be separated, except through PEDs, which his channel rejects. {{_Af_kG__xVg@879}}
- [1v 2020 O] What dictated the fight was spirit, not physical differences: the one who always found the strength to stand up triumphed {{V6YDUJZ2E64@1030}}
- [1v 2021 P] Action always beats thinking: if forced to choose between physical and mental, the physical produces more gains, and the mental is how you feed the fire intelligently {{tLvshnh6vZA@370}}
- [1v 2021 P] The more you practise stepping back, the easier it gets; rushing into solutions makes it harder {{fsyx5y-VYns@752}}
- [1v 2022 O] Bullying either creates broken people or extremely tough ones, with little in between {{Du0CfRzLfTg@948}}
- [1v 2022 O] Cyberbullying may be worse than physical bullying because you can't fight back physically {{-64JDG_Aoio@2291}}
- [1v 2022 O] Most bullying is psychological; physical bullying is the easier kind to deal with {{-64JDG_Aoio@1369}}
- [1v 2022 P] Bullying either forges or breaks you, like tempering or shattering a sword {{-64JDG_Aoio@1328}}
- [1v 2024 O] Stating your superiority outright is arrogant; people respond to trauma differently and a teacher must deal with people who are not hardened like him {{qYaWxvA4fKg@503}}
- [1v 2024 A] Random anger bursts usually come from trauma; physical bullying when young means he still goes into fight mode when someone shoves or slaps him {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@10064}}
- [1v 2024 P] Verbal bullying is as damaging as physical; respond verbally to verbal aggression and dish it back {{Plr3f3LXO_o@8943}}
- [1v 2025 A] He beat bullying by learning from a friend that you must appear crazy and retaliate instantly whenever someone messes with you {{0O0nG2jLadU@7682}}

## answer / question / ask / whether (10)
- [2v 2022 P] Most people avoid struggle, and their default answer to whether they want to train is no, so stop asking yourself the question and do not ask for your own consent also: 2022-02: Don't ask whether you want to go to the gym but whether you have to; the answer is always yes, and soon you stop asking {{U_4FtEW5nh0@1}} {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@941}}
- [1v 2020 P] His definition of an intermediate: someone who can answer the question 'why do you lift?' {{K9hKDPaDFsI@374}}
- [1v 2020 P] For every practice in your bodybuilding training, ask why you do it; if you cannot answer, you probably succumbed to peer pressure {{iEKSZ806mrc@862}}
- [1v 2020 P] Work is the answer to everything, and you keep working until you die {{xCzoFKSPkIc@1827}}
- [1v 2021 P] Always ask how something benefits you; the group needs you, not the other way around {{j1bS37-7AHA@5543}}
- [1v 2021 O] Asking whether lifting is worth it is pointless, because the time would otherwise be wasted on unproductive things {{yr4zj-WbG4I@469}}
- [1v 2021 P] To fight gluttony, question your consumption: ask why you consume each thing and reject intruders {{FxqDfwz_A-g@377}}
- [1v 2022 P] Training should be scheduled like lunch so there is never a question of whether you will train {{he2KJeMcoAI@2017}}
- [1v 2022 P] Cultivate some cynical traits (without becoming a Cynic or bitter) to question everything and keep intellectual honesty {{jkQHeNZIHnY@1589}}
- [1v 2024 O] He deliberately avoids giving clear-cut answers because developing the ability to find the answer is what makes you better, not the answer itself {{fPU3nRIH8sM@973}}

## clear / goal / strength / ones (11)
- [1v 2020 A] He has clear, detailed physique goals rather than vague ones. {{KlSyeS0R9FE@190}}
- [1v 2020 P] Having a long-term goal is the most important thing; the novice phase should open windows onto what you want to pursue next (aesthetics, strength, athletic prowess), not just a number like a 45-inch chest. {{K9hKDPaDFsI@313}}
- [1v 2020 P] You need to know the destination even if you don't know the exact route; the path is discovered along the way, but without a clear goal you can't stay on it. {{QTxLU8WTx6g@570}}
- [1v 2020 P] Do not build anxiety about threats with no clear sign; prevent what you know is coming and ignore what you cannot foresee {{RLwjGoejUXk@1453}}
- [1v 2021 P] Problems look huge up close; step back and treat them like a nightmare you wake from, then work on them with a clear mind {{xGoJNq78htU@566}}
- [1v 2021 P] You need a clear goal (strength, aesthetics, calisthenics, sport) to give your training direction {{8ELQ2XZgpls@850}}
- [1v 2021 P] Many people reason clearly about others' crises but lose their logic when they are the ones on fire {{TD_WzcWEmFg@752}}
- [1v 2021 P] Lift with a clear goal; without one, progress itself becomes the goal and you lose the plot {{aIjUTEtXOcM@1565}}
- [1v 2021 P] People with a clearly defined goal body they deeply want are the ones who stay consistent {{PYICG0hBlVo@1764}}
- [1v 2023 P] Decide what you want your 'statue' to look like (or whether you prefer strength); a clear why makes sticking to the how possible {{16fjHEtZTCA@1839}}

- [1v 2021 O] Everyone is a dopamine junkie; being a slave to dopamine is the quickest way to ruin your life {{P5N7KRKl3c0@1135}}
- [1v 2021 O] Lifting floods you with dopamine and endorphins; NH feels he can now trigger good feelings at will, and lifting helps depression and anxiety {{yr4zj-WbG4I@2066}}
- [1v 2021 O] Lifting reduces your need for approval, which makes you more socially at ease {{yr4zj-WbG4I@2125}}
- [1v 2022 O] Lifting would fix most psychological problems such as social anxiety, which he used to suffer from {{JNBgBfmTqx8@1761}}
- [1v 2024 O] Consuming motivation content gives a dopamine spike that substitutes for actually acting, so people stay passive {{Dciz-VeNHVI@1826}}
- [1v 2024 P] Social anxiety with strangers comes from predicting how they will judge you; stay in the moment even if you look foolish {{jmOPC_ozpwc@2832}}

- [1v 2024 P] Your circumstances don't matter in the end: you are responsible for your own happiness and future {{yX1br2PHa0w@3501}}
- [1v 2025 P] Resentment, regardless of source or sex, is always destructive because it stops self-reflection and focuses on external factors {{kptuCb6_2Cc@4043}}

- [1v 2022 P] Every difficulty is your responsibility because only you can react to it; laziness is a developed trait, not your DNA, and you are not a loser, just someone who loses {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@2560}}

- [1v 2024 P] See your body as a vessel for the self, a project permanently under construction that can always improve {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@441}}

- [1v 2022 A] Bugenhagen's excesses (eating garlic until he puked, drinking raw honey, blowing out his knees twice) are self-destructive but part of an all-in path NH respects {{kKX0AUrXzIw@1512}}
- [1v 2024 O] Lack of boundaries also ruins potential relationships, friendships and romantic interests, since people lose respect for you {{v1URYz8VF5E@316}}
- [1v 2024 P] Respect and learn from him, but don't idolize him or anyone; he is just a regular guy {{TvKzeUKgZcY@1897}}

## gratification / instant / delay / dopamine (7)
- [1v 2021 P] Instant gratification is the greatest trap: taking the pleasure before doing the work means the work never gets done, and any dopamine you get without paying is billed later {{P5N7KRKl3c0@506}}
- [1v 2021 P] Constantly ask why you are drawn to something: the caveman brain chases sex, food and instant pleasure that no longer serve survival, and questioning it builds a strong anti-hedonistic mind {{P5N7KRKl3c0@1072}}
- [1v 2021 P] If something is truly worth pursuing, its payback will not come tomorrow; delayed gratification marks worthwhile goals {{P5N7KRKl3c0@1009}}
- [1v 2021 P] Human bodies are wired for dopamine and instant gratification; delayed gratification is the self-control that lifts us above animals {{Da6d5cecRKA@167}}
- [1v 2022 P] Rejecting instant gratification is a key part of developing yourself as a man {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@254}}
- [1v 2022 P] Impatience and weakness to instant gratification make natural lifting a self-fulfilling failure {{ihFhv7P3wpI@170}}
- [1v 2022 P] Strength gives instant gratification as weight on the bar, so when chasing both it naturally takes over your training {{JgAoGDmM4n4@1626}}

## confidence / bring / attention / key (7)
- [1v 2020 O] Deep confidence makes you immune to attacks and sets an example that people lacking confidence will copy {{2PyZ82unsj8@125}}
- [1v 2021 P] Do not let confidence degenerate into the domineering, attention-seeking stereotype of the alpha {{Rf00mkH4QkQ@1015}}
- [1v 2022 P] Stoicism is the key to confidence because it refocuses attention on yourself {{eC9Srr3ZygE@727}}
- [1v 2022 P] A strong, resilient body is the key to independence and brings unmatched confidence {{3W-yE2pgTec@758}}
- [1v 2023 O] Confidence is the main thing missing from the Asian men he meets; reclaiming pride brings confidence and confident people can be grateful {{wE3vuAA7K1Y@1127}}
- [1v 2023 P] 'Pathological stoicism' is the compulsive rejection of all feelings for a fake stability; it brings back anxiety, depression and low confidence {{YzE7sjeMFCs@1270}}
- [1v 2024 P] Stop clicking and sharing outrage content and thirst traps; for those creators all attention is good attention {{Dciz-VeNHVI@885}}

## willpower / each / force / reinforce (7)
- [1v 2022 P] Willpower is built by training: each time you force yourself to get something done you reinforce it, until it becomes seamless and is no longer needed {{kKX0AUrXzIw@1006}}
- [1v 2022 O] Most natural lifters never put in the mandatory 10 years because they rely on discipline to force themselves to do something they don't like {2022-05 {"years": 10}} {{kKX0AUrXzIw@2323}}
- [1v 2022 O] Willpower is declining with each generation, which makes now the moment with the highest incentive to build it {{kKX0AUrXzIw@190}}
- [1v 2022 P] Willpower is trainable: each time you push through you teach yourself to win, each time you quit for a bad reason you reinforce weakness {{vdEfTs-aMmM@316}}
- [1v 2023 P] You don't need superhuman willpower to start; advanced lifters weren't special, they decided to be, and body and willpower build each other {{l2tFBCf8cdg@4511}}
- [1v 2024 P] Willpower is finite but trainable; every time you give in to a temptation like Nutella you teach yourself your will is weak, and every resistance strengthens it {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@1094}}
- [1v 2024 P] If you struggle to make friends in college, force yourself to socialize; the longer you stay isolated the harder it becomes {{wBct3Pl8OY4@893}}

## version / best / asking / himself (7)
- [1v 2021 O] He wants to be the best version of himself by his own definition, not the best at bodybuilding {{QTHtVBP_36g@2583}}
- [1v 2021 O] A serious lifter's duty is to provoke non-lifters (DYELs) into training and becoming bigger versions of themselves {{Ugzb5KfZnKnYoBUjS614AaABCQ@0}}
- [1v 2022 P] Use idols as inspiration but never copy them; asking 'can I look like him' is pointless, you can only be the best version of you {{kKX0AUrXzIw@691}}
- [1v 2022 P] Stop asking how long until you look like someone; a physique is not built overnight {{ihFhv7P3wpI@190}}
- [1v 2023 P] Self-worth should not depend on others' approval; you owe it to yourself to become your best version {{zuvTDBbAWN8@1449}}
- [1v 2024 O] People who downplay body dysmorphic disorder are wrong; he has dealt with a mild version himself {{yX1br2PHa0w@1275}}
- [1v 2024 P] To accept that a better version of you exists you must accept your current version is incomplete, which many people cannot do {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@313}}

## stand / cannot / recognize / change (7)
- [1v 2020 P] Courage is not an action but the absence of fleeing or renouncing: simply stand your ground and endure {{RLwjGoejUXk@505}}
- [1v 2022 P] Make bullies pay a price; physical injuries heal but the self-worth lost by never standing up for yourself doesn't {{-64JDG_Aoio@2135}}
- [1v 2022 P] You can only change your standing in a hierarchy once you recognize that it exists {{rGMi5_hLRH8@2414}}
- [1v 2023 O] People tear down statues because they cannot stand the comparison with their ancestors; the Spartans faced death smiling while you cannot get off the couch to train {{TPcnP8Tvj5E@1948}}
- [1v 2024 O] A man who cannot stand up for himself is unattractive to women because he shows he could not stand up for them {{v1URYz8VF5E@379}}
- [1v 2024 P] The fitness community is plagued by body dysmorphia because it is never satisfied; accept what you cannot change and work on what you can {{qYaWxvA4fKg@1580}}
- [1v 2024 P] For worries you cannot control, stoicism helps: recognize you have no control and let it go to focus on what you can change {{wBct3Pl8OY4@3936}}

## negativity / fake / accomplishment / act (7)
- [1v 2020 P] Dedicated lifters do not attack other disciplines; convinced of their own path, they feel no need to convince others or feed negativity {{mO3cqgBEqdc@1201}}
- [1v 2020 O] Many 'fake powerlifters' secretly want aesthetics but settle for strength because it comes easily and earns praise, leaving them bitter and passionless {{-1dECrWYeFk@620}}
- [1v 2022 P] Feeling superior through knowledge you never act on is fake; only action and accomplishment count {{_KT_9vmHNzE@942}}
- [1v 2022 P] Real negativity that makes you uncomfortable and pushes you to act is useful; fake negativity keeps you static {{vdEfTs-aMmM@2448}}
- [1v 2022 P] Lifting satisfies you through accomplishment; purchased bodies never fill the void and create dependence like PEDs do {{crP2w97XXfs@1070}}
- [1v 2023 P] Same water that softens the potato hardens the egg: some people thrive on negativity, others need positive reinforcement {{kXdKs8Fu2WU@1833}}
- [1v 2024 P] Do not dodge real-life negativity, but do not import extra negativity from strangers online; it creates a cycle of desperation {{Dciz-VeNHVI@506}}

## emotion / repress / accept / understand (7)
- [1v 2021 O] Take the objective information from praise and criticism but bear the emotions they provoke; he still enjoys compliments and is working on it {{4NzW3-pvuGM@507}}
- [1v 2021 P] To control your emotions you must first accept them, and to not be a victim of violence you must embrace it; running from fears only strengthens them {{Rf00mkH4QkQ@63}}
- [1v 2021 P] The situation is neutral: two people in the same situation react differently, so the emotion comes from you; easy to understand, hard to apply {{_k8bxXE1-Jk@192}}
- [1v 2022 P] Stoicism means controlling emotions after accepting them, not repressing them; repressed feelings drive behavior from the unconscious and often come out as anger {{ttUzFhGo6Bc@2407}}
- [1v 2023 P] To master your emotions you must first understand and accept them; rejecting them means you aren't even working with the material Stoicism needs {{YzE7sjeMFCs@1144}}
- [1v 2023 O] Righteous anger is important today; the line between controlling emotions and being a coward is very thin {{YzE7sjeMFCs@954}}
- [1v 2024 O] The pathological stoic who represses all emotion cuts himself off from himself; manosphere voices wrongly equate self-improvement with becoming a statue {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@2456}}

## gymcel / committ / clock / check (7)
- [1v 2020 P] Musashi's 'make fullest use of your weaponry': once committed, fight to the fullest rather than dying with your sword unused. {{jA3PfQmxrYA@955}}
- [1v 2022 O] Stop asking how long it will take; skinny men who keep checking the clock are not committed {{wB-ydKSfQ5k@2209}}
- [1v 2023 O] Ridicule of obsessive lifters as 'gymcels' comes from mediocre men who never committed to anything {{M2yqxp4IHXg@2075}}
- [1v 2024 P] Dedication is healthy; obsession is gymcel behavior. Checking the clock to leave is a sign something is wrong {{lGbT0aT2hOk@1629}}
- [1v 2024 O] Using 'gymcel' or 'try-hard' to mock people passionate about fitness is projection by people who can't commit to anything; self-improvement beats passive scrolling {{lGbT0aT2hOk@0}}
- [1v 2024 O] If you only feel good while training and the depression returns afterwards, you are in a gymcel cycle; the solution lies elsewhere {{lyj1BFZ85Dg@1094}}
- [1v 2024 P] Men who stop lifting once they get a girlfriend used it only as a tool to attract women; lifting is for self-improvement, and she may leave once the body goes {{jmOPC_ozpwc@4046}}

## hardship / unavoidable / accept / willingly (5)
- [2v 2021-2023 P] Strength can only be built through struggle; pain is unavoidable, so you either accept it willingly or become its victim also: 2021-09: Accept unavoidable hardships willingly and with a smile; it makes them easier {{0zEVs8KDOPY@2556}} {{TPcnP8Tvj5E@242}}
- [1v 2020 P] If you accept hardship from the start it stops being hardship; that's why training is doable and even enjoyable {{Qgpc7evE7_4@504}}
- [1v 2020 P] Men who never endured hardship lack resilience at their core; adversity is best met while young {{RLwjGoejUXk@190}}
- [1v 2020 P] The worst thing in a crisis is being taken by surprise; at least picture and think through hardship in advance {{xoxYiiTPdIs@813}}
- [1v 2021 P] You can't only train or study when motivated; betterment must be scheduled and unavoidable, which is why accountability matters in lifting {{2qPocEhaU0I@1131}}

## bulk / path / resistance / dirty (6)
- [1v 2020 O] Dirty bulking is lazy, the easy way out and the path of least resistance. {{a02c10s3Pjk@568}}
- [1v 2020 P] Fears and insecurities exist only because you give them life; chase them off your path and make them obey your will {{8ur4A9N_h1c@1020}}
- [1v 2020 P] Many people waste time changing paths and seeking least resistance; decide, do it fully and do not give up {{24j6eNqmnZ0@439}}
- [1v 2021 P] Whether a lifting journey succeeds and lasts depends on forming your own connection with lifting, more than on optimal gains, bulking vs cutting, or program choice {{j1bS37-7AHA@1385}}
- [1v 2021 O] Body dysmorphia levels have skyrocketed compared to 30 years ago because of the modern media environment {2021-11 {"years": 30}} {{Tos7dHreF3k@375}}
- [1v 2023 A] The dirty bulk about five years ago gave him lasting body dysmorphia (bigorexia): he still feels not big enough {{XK9KjVP-5Wo@1003}}

## prophecy / stagnation / self-fulfill / maintain (6)
- [1v 2020 P] Deciding you can only maintain after 30 makes you cut volume or intensity until stagnation is all that happens: a self-fulfilling prophecy. {{TIsnQ03FegQ@37}}
- [1v 2021 P] Disagree openly, without the deferential 'respectfully' and without pre-emptive aggression, which becomes a self-fulfilling prophecy {{j1bS37-7AHA@5446}}
- [1v 2021 P] Stagnation doesn't exist for humans: if you stop pushing for more, you regress, so maintaining forever is a bad mindset {{PYICG0hBlVo@0}}
- [1v 2021 P] Stagnation is death: stop challenging yourself and you regress, and reverse momentum is hard to stop, but momentum toward virtue also builds {{0zEVs8KDOPY@601}}
- [1v 2022 O] Deciding you are doomed is a self-fulfilling prophecy; today's 'reasonable' man is often a coward {{SDVF9vWBQAM@1007}}
- [1v 2023 P] Training is a battle needing strategy (the program: volume, intensity, frequency) and technique (execution); both must be under control {{1apseBbt0-o@0}}

## consistently / noth / hard / bad (6)
- [1v 2020 P] Saying you can't achieve anything without drugs or with bad genetics is dishonest; the Way is in training, and believing training does nothing makes it so. Train hard and consistently and let the way find you. {{KadH0ZYObS0@880}}
- [1v 2020 P] The value of hard work is among the most important things to learn; once you see years of effort pay off you know you can achieve anything {{vZUEtun_5no@822}}
- [1v 2020 P] Demoralization is the bodybuilder's enemy; believing in bad genetics stops people from realizing their potential {{xCzoFKSPkIc@0}}
- [1v 2021 P] Years of training mean nothing on their own; what matters is how long you've trained hard and consistently {{K7cehT_XyBQ@815}}
- [1v 2022 O] NH guesses (no source) that park trainees train more consistently than people paying for commercial gyms {{6YWa3qOu6BY@701}}
- [1v 2024 P] To overcome shyness deliberately make conversations cringe and see that nothing bad happens {{bRKQIOYo2u4@7977}}

## run / taste / long / stamina (6)
- [1v 2020 P] Give novices just a small taste of the gym each week so they are eager to come back rather than dreading it; the aim is discipline. {{QpTHtmI8nrU@317}}
- [1v 2020 P] Thinking in five-year horizons is pointless; you're in it for the long run, and if the fast guy stalls after two years, reaching his level in five means you then pass him. {2020-02 {"weeks": "2 vs 5 years"}} {{QiOh8SF-UxQ@427}}
- [1v 2020 P] Better to taste the pain of discipline early than to see a chunk of your life disappear; lifting is a long run and you need to live long for that. {{woBGrw0JWvo@378}}
- [1v 2020 O] 'I don't have time to train' is a lie for most people; once you develop a taste for hard work you need it in your life {{mO3cqgBEqdc@445}}
- [1v 2021 P] Mental stamina will limit you in the long run; train mind as well as body {{8ELQ2XZgpls@2479}}
- [1v 2021 O] Perfection being unreachable is a blessing because it means you never run out of reasons to train {{PYICG0hBlVo@1388}}

## cold / fight-or / flight / trigger (6)
- [1v 2020 P] The most underrated benefit of cold showers is mental fortitude: they trigger fight-or-flight and anxious anticipation you learn to overcome. {{mfweKhLM5L0@626}}
- [1v 2020 O] Cold showers are a free habit that builds mental strength and aids recovery; once established you don't think twice about it. {{mfweKhLM5L0@877}}
- [1v 2020 P] Start with the body, then the head; cold water on the face triggers a much stronger, exhilarating fight-or-flight reaction. {{mfweKhLM5L0@689}}
- [1v 2020 P] Start gradually; tap water at home isn't that cold, but stream water is much colder and gives less time to adapt. {{mfweKhLM5L0@689}}
- [1v 2021 O] The link between physique and mental strength and well-being is the most underrated benefit of staying natural {{tY9nfPVh4WE@115}}
- [1v 2022 O] The squat triggers a life-or-death fight-or-flight response with the bar on your back that builds mental strength carrying over to life {{gYyqqbs2SLI@316}}

- [1v 2020 A] The strength of mind he built as a slow-progressing skinny teen made up for his slow physical progress. {{QTxLU8WTx6g@378}}
- [1v 2020 P] Many men start lifting out of insecurity because their body doesn't match the image they want, or they believe others judge them for being skinny or fat. {{StpfmIPa2BY@0}}

- [1v 2024 O] Lifters who say ten years of training was all for nothing are rejecting real progress because it does not match a grandiose image; you are owed nothing for trying {2024-06 {"years": 10}} {{AZSZmhOJZJI@1901}}
- [1v 2024 O] Most people are skinny or fat because both result from lack of physical activity and discipline {{fPU3nRIH8sM@9992}}

## watch / video / else' / vicariously (6)
- [1v 2020 O] Living vicariously through someone else's body or strength is a waste of life; he would rather you train for five minutes than watch one of his five-minute videos {2020-07 {"duration": "5 min"}} {{zt2-wI7GJ-c@63}}
- [1v 2021 P] Choose your own reason for lifting; many quit after years of training for someone else's goals {{9mnLRQsrs9k@1184}}
- [1v 2021 O] Watching fake natties makes viewers jaded: they decide the physique is unreachable without drugs and their self-esteem collapses {{CcKOfe_nznI@775}}
- [1v 2021 O] Watching sports for hours without playing any is passive avoidance passed off as a manly activity {{OMoyytRioRg@833}}
- [1v 2022 P] Training videos should be the spark that gets you training, not a substitute; many viewers live vicariously through other people's workouts {{rVy03_C5jEU@314}}
- [1v 2022 O] Watching his lifting videos without training is like watching someone make soup {{eWP2m4lLq_0@1387}}

## visualization / prayer / manifestation / action (6)
- [1v 2020 O] Visualization works only when paired with action, like prayer must be accompanied by effort {{24j6eNqmnZ0@502}}
- [1v 2020 P] Manifestation is not a prayer: you go out and get it {{kh--k3genZA@250}}
- [1v 2020 P] The solution is not meditation, prayer or reading but action: train day in and day out regardless of doubts {{4xgx3q5a1DQ@270}}
- [1v 2021 A] Reeves used visualization, picturing his muscle fibers working and growing in an almost hypnotic focus {{qcRCMVABEQM@735}}
- [1v 2023 P] Visualization without action is nothing; together they create manifestation {{UWr5TQVT63g@1067}}

## proces / obstacle / enjoy / enjoyment (6)
- [1v 2020 P] The best way to keep training forever is to enjoy the process; without enjoyment you'll quit at the first obstacle {{JXVJtNrIZ9U@62}}
- [1v 2020 P] Enjoy training; you get more results when you stop obsessing over results because you can focus on the process {{vZUEtun_5no@698}}
- [1v 2021 O] People who skip training the moment an obstacle appears (car breaks, so no gym) were looking for an excuse; bike or run there instead {{ijchEb7aoBg@1196}}

- [1v 2022 P] Like a tempered sword, you need discipline and flexibility together or you break at the first obstacle {{6HGBdDOAReA@506}}
- [1v 2024 P] Enjoyment ranks 1st and trumps every objective metric: a lift you don't enjoy gets skipped and done without intensity, so it gives no progress {{6yW3GSskl8g@1645}}

## yet / simple / have / walk (6)
- [1v 2020 P] If you cannot yet keep a routine, build it in small steps: shower, get dressed, take a two-minute walk, then add more {{ewvy5V03gTE@252}}
- [1v 2020 P] Humans evolved to seek results, yet unlike animals we can keep doing something that returns nothing, such as loving a discipline we have no talent for {{mO3cqgBEqdc@700}}
- [1v 2020 P] Bouts of dysmorphia should be accepted as a simple recognition that you have not arrived yet; keep walking toward your goal daily {{MuQvx5dNpno@655}}
- [1v 2022 P] Programming is simple if you allow yourself to be wrong; the simple approach is as effective as the galaxy-brain calculation and is the one he recommends {{iDua_rNsBO8@1506}}
- [1v 2023 O] Advanced natural lifters are all quirky individuals; novices form an undifferentiated mass because they don't yet know what they want {{l2tFBCf8cdg@4720}}
- [1v 2024 P] To find a meaning not yet in your life you must do things you have never done {{1bCQ2ezQfAA@4053}}

## rather / focu / youtube / hero (6)
- [1v 2021 O] Looking at women in yoga pants is not a testosterone boost; it shifts your focus off the weights {{h21r7Mrxfo8@688}}
- [1v 2021 P] Focus on yourself, your training and your life rather than YouTube heroes {{JpSZFxsTyKk@2280}}
- [1v 2021 P] Treat setbacks as opportunities to learn rather than slights {{2qPocEhaU0I@879}}
- [1v 2022 O] Most lifters undertrain rather than overtrain, because fear-mongering presents a hospitalization worst case that does not actually exist {{NPlGK0-7h0A@1252}}
- [1v 2022 P] YouTube fitness is an environment in which creators and viewers shape each other in a feedback loop; many viewers' goals are borrowed from creators rather than their own {{EdfnJor78OQ@313}}
- [1v 2022 P] Practise gratitude: focus on what you can influence rather than what you lack {{vdEfTs-aMmM@1819}}

## food / junk / eat / overconsumption (6)
- [1v 2021 O] Nobody forces you to eat junk food; blaming a partner for your weight gain shows weakness {{-c9oXlYva3k@2449}}
- [1v 2021 O] Modern distractions (phones, sugary food, porn) always give you an easier option than changing {{OMoyytRioRg@895}}
- [1v 2021 P] The company you keep matters more than the food you eat; the more acquaintances, the more danger, so vet and limit them {{7ddQIjZoujQ@1327}}
- [1v 2021 P] Gluttony in fitness is overconsumption of anything, not only food {{FxqDfwz_A-g@62}}
- [1v 2022 P] Lifters, like religious practitioners, give up drinking, junk food and comfort because they know the sacrifice pays off later {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@222}}
- [1v 2023 O] Claims that this generation is doomed by plastics in food and crushed testosterone are excuses people use to explain why they suck {{wE3vuAA7K1Y@1316}}

## comparison / focu / effort / vacuum (6)
- [1v 2020 P] Non-depressed lifters should focus on training and recovery and not obsess over metabolism or low testosterone; depressed lifters should do the opposite {{ewvy5V03gTE@694}}
- [1v 2020 O] If a belief needs to silence or ignore others, it is not sound; a training method that only works in a vacuum and wobbles under comparison is a bad method {{Lg-w_lgCgUc@251}}
- [1v 2021 O] Wasted effort is a concept born from comparison; focus on yourself {{9mnLRQsrs9k@3194}}
- [1v 2021 P] Body/muscle dysmorphia is not born in a vacuum; it arises from comparison with others and is passed person to person like a contagious disease in the fitness community {{Tos7dHreF3k@160}}
- [1v 2023 P] Visualize the target muscle stretching and contracting while training it; biologically meaningless but it boosts belief, effort and consistency {{UWr5TQVT63g@1317}}
- [1v 2024 P] Someone fit and strong but more depressed than ever spent effort in the wrong direction and needs to focus on the spiritual {{fPU3nRIH8sM@9862}}

- [1v 2021 O] Calling a return to masturbation a 'relapse' borrows drug-addiction language and shows the sex drive has been demonized {{IPSRlANKpDw@3728}}
- [1v 2022 P] If school is too easy, make your own challenge: aim to be the best, learn a language on your own or read during class {{eC9Srr3ZygE@343}}

- [1v 2025 A] Reading philosophy concepts for 10 minutes every night all year let him ace a surprise test the rest of the class failed {{0O0nG2jLadU@8689}}

## strength / care / look / important (6)
- [1v 2020 O] For NH, strength of mind is as important as strength of body. {{_m2GZc7fE-A@565}}
- [1v 2022 P] Self-test: if a PR excites you most you care about strength; if a new muscle or vein in the mirror does, you care about looks. Five pounds on the bar means nothing to him {{GNJkAcgUJ_0@4078}}
- [1v 2022 P] If you care about looks, call yourself a (natural) bodybuilder instead of hiding behind labels like powerbuilder {{GNJkAcgUJ_0@5458}}
- [1v 2023 P] Pursue strength but know why; without a why you end up with strength you have no use for, as Baki did {{sgQD0WIfuvc@2914}}
- [1v 2023 A] Be honest with yourself about whether you care about strength or size; NH felt nothing hitting strength PRs and is driven by seeing his body change {{M2yqxp4IHXg@1444}}
- [1v 2024 O] Bodybuilding works like a martial art: looking powerful means people do not mess with you whether or not you can fight {{wBct3Pl8OY4@3045}}

## pain / poison / itself / weak (6)
- [1v 2021 P] Control pain in reverse order: first stop saying you are hurt out loud, since whining only makes you look weak, then stop the thought itself {{5dUZbDUyifU@64}}
- [1v 2021 P] Things you get without pain or sacrifice are usually not worth it and can even be poison; virtue has to be worked for {{Da6d5cecRKA@1172}}
- [1v 2021 O] Destigmatize the person, not the illness; normalizing BDD itself removes the drive to fix it, and the pain is the signal the problem remains {{Tos7dHreF3k@1427}}
- [1v 2022 P] One value for a man is poison to another: refusing to see pain as bad works for the strong but breaks the weak, who want to be protected from pain {{AJ8AhxJWas8@2180}}
- [1v 2023 P] Strength is always the solution in life; the pain of changing yourself lasts months or years while the pain of staying weak lasts forever {{wE3vuAA7K1Y@1066}}
- [1v 2024 P] Pain is unpleasant, but the consequences of never facing pain are far worse than the pain itself; realizing this is when a boy becomes a man {{AZSZmhOJZJI@828}}

## minut / active / 45 / day (6)
- [1v 2022 P] Scheduled active rest, like 45 minutes on the couch after a long day, is not wasted time; unscheduled procrastination is {2022-07 {"rest": "45 min"}} {{u8IRHcUWfPs@186}}
- [1v 2022 O] People who say they are too busy to lift usually waste hours, such as 45 minutes scrolling and an hour of Netflix a day {{u8IRHcUWfPs@312}}

## apply / bicep / microplastic / addictive (5)
- [1v 2020 P] Information you don't apply is worthless: reading a two-hour paper on biceps growth without training biceps is a waste of life; applying knowledge makes it your own and lets you pass it on. {{j2u5TzD5fGg@189}}

## shout / timid / kick / three (5)
- [1v 2020 O] Musashi's three shouts map onto lifting as shouting before the set, on each rep and after; commercial gyms will kick you out but a home gym allows it {{swSh89g9Gts@440}}
- [1v 2020 P] Shouting in the middle of a tough set re-invigorates you and makes the rest easier, while a timid half-shout can psych you out {{swSh89g9Gts@316}}
- [1v 2020 P] Shouting costs mental stamina and spikes adrenaline, which can leave you depleted like too much coffee, so use it only when necessary {{swSh89g9Gts@440}}
- [1v 2020 P] What you repeat to yourself becomes your identity: acting timid in small things primes you to be timid when it matters {{JXVJtNrIZ9U@693}}

- [1v 2020 P] Do not jump to every new program he publishes; stick with one and eventually make your own {{1f4aaqgOfXk@353}}

- [1v 2022 P] The loser's formula: jump in with no method, quit when it sucks, lose self-worth and become more likely to quit next time; the winner's formula is sticking with it, seeing results and gaining motivation {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@467}}

- [1v 2020 P] Hate aimed at someone who does not know you exist would make you far happier if spent lifting {{zt2-wI7GJ-c@248}}
- [1v 2020 P] Anxiety is energy; if it isn't spent it will destroy you {{zAUmHWbOywA@0}}
- [1v 2020 P] Modern desk jobs exhaust the mind but leave the body full of energy, which creates anxiety; training fixes this {{zAUmHWbOywA@252}}

## push-up / rock / mountain / homework (5)

- [1v 2020 P] Hard work is realized one step at a time: 100 days of training is daunting but you can always endure one day, like moving a mountain one rock at a time {2020-10 {"days": 100}} {{RLwjGoejUXk@694}}
