- [1v 2020 P] Training is its own pursuit: the exertion itself is the goal, even when it doesn't produce the results you want {{JXVJtNrIZ9U@0}}
- [1v 2020 P] Strength progress fuels the drive for results but must never become the goal itself {{ISLtO-xJbYo@317}}
- [1v 2021 O] His goal in lifting is not athleticism or strength but to become a beast, a wall of muscle with as much lean mass as possible {{ivrGNRW0rwE@0}}
- [1v 2023 P] An 'aristocracy of the body' is earned through the choice of physical exertion, not inherited, and is lost if you stop lifting {{l2tFBCf8cdg@2910}}

## futile / secret / person / eliminate (4)
- [1v 2020 P] Trying to eliminate the dysmorphic drive is futile; you must collaborate with it because the person you became exists because of it {{MuQvx5dNpno@230}}
- [1v 2022 O] You should know yourself better than anyone; even his closest person knows him only about 60-70%, and keeping a secret garden is human, not dishonest {2022-03 {"percent": "60-70%"}} {{Du0CfRzLfTg@569}}
- [1v 2022 P] Secret hope that someone will come save you is futile: that person is you {{nZnt4--nIpY@1020}}
- [1v 2022 P] Trying to cut off sensual inclinations entirely fails because they resurface elsewhere; the goal is to control them, not eliminate them {{AJ8AhxJWas8@2873}}

## behaviour / bad / eat / sleep (4)
- [1v 2021 P] Skipping training because you failed to eat or sleep reinforces bad behaviour; training is non-negotiable {{xlarSnYhRVc@928}}
- [1v 2022 P] Link training to positive habits like eating, a warm bath or a TV show; bad habits pair the same way, like cigarettes with coffee {{Jdv6dG_Stzw@1314}}
- [1v 2022 P] Gamers either forget to eat and drink or snack on soda and chips; bad habits come in pairs and gaming ends up costing sleep, diet, social life and training sessions {{bM23N1IPrDk@3029}}
- [1v 2023 P] To convince anyone to change behaviour you must articulate concretely why it benefits them and show the future consequences, not just call things good or bad {{bntFiuquKGI@1255}}

## transform / receive / event / vial (4)
- [1v 2021 P] Treat hardship as something you receive and transform rather than as a victim {{4NzW3-pvuGM@129}}
- [1v 2021 P] When suffering, focusing on yourself makes time slow down; what hurts is mostly how you receive the event, not the event itself {{Uzm8hsjuYcY@200}}
- [1v 2021 O] Gains from a vial rob you of the pride of having transformed yourself {{McAet6dp70U@1378}}
- [1v 2024 P] When you meet people from your past after transforming, they see the new you; ride the confidence you earned rather than shrinking {{wBct3Pl8OY4@2543}}

## craft / surrender / idoliz / anyone (4)
- [1v 2020 P] Idolizing people creates an artificial ceiling that blinds you to anyone surpassing it {{uL-pwl2nfa0@256}}
- [1v 2021 P] What you do in training matters less than doing it and surrendering to the craft even if not optimal; anyone can change {{ivrGNRW0rwE@565}}
- [1v 2021 P] Fanboyism is idolizing someone other than yourself and surrendering your identity to a perceived group; you lose agency and become passive {{j1bS37-7AHA@98}}
- [1v 2022 P] Pursuing any craft requires lots of time alone, which is torture if your self is empty {{Du0CfRzLfTg@63}}

## useful / event / forc / harmful (4)
- [1v 2021 O] Humans perceive natural events as personal challenges, which is both useful (we build lasting shelters) and harmful (victim mentality) {{lsMyNBeNPvk@632}}
- [1v 2021 O] Forced positivity and forced negativity are both harmful; negativity is a useful force, but emotions should be restrained to where they are useful {{tLvshnh6vZA@1276}}
- [1v 2021 P] Only engage with forces you intend to destroy or fix; otherwise move on without a single thought, since you are the only one keeping the event alive {{Uzm8hsjuYcY@500}}
- [1v 2021 P] Visualisation is useful for preparation, but don't mentally live out events before they happen {{as17cE3iIy0@1394}}

## finger / project / side / partly (4)
- [1v 2020 O] He partly disagrees with 'do nothing which is of no use': he does fun projects like finger push-ups that won't make him bigger, but such distractions should be kept to a minimum if you want to master something. {{KadH0ZYObS0@1195}}
- [1v 2021 P] Add a small side project for fun, about 1% of total work, such as his finger push-ups, which do little for hypertrophy {{0RoULYC_tao@686}}
- [1v 2021 P] You can't be a really good bodybuilder and a really good boxer at once; pick one main pursuit and keep side projects small {{0RoULYC_tao@748}}
- [1v 2023 P] Fingers opening on towel pull-ups means undertrained fingers and is partly mental; squeeze like your life depends on it {{kQMrIszr_Bw@1197}}

## face / sensation / break / melt (4)
- [1v 2022 O] No challenge you face is truly impossible; pain is an internal sensation, so you alone can defeat it {{0UJ1W5yogoQ@845}}
- [1v 2022 O] Strong online personalities that melt down face to face reveal identities that were never strong {{Du0CfRzLfTg@1135}}
- [1v 2022 P] Escapism is fine as a short break before you face the problem, but if you never face it the break stops being recovery and becomes cowardice {{bM23N1IPrDk@569}}
- [1v 2024 A] Two weeks in grad school without speaking to anyone made him dissociate until his own name and face felt foreign; humans need social contact {2024-12 {"weeks": 2}} {{TvKzeUKgZcY@11257}}

## competition / self-improvement / toxic / media (4)
- [1v 2022 O] Mogging is mostly a pursuit of self-improvement; comparison and competition are healthy sources of motivation, not toxic {{ZPBwBrOktgA@1009}}
- [1v 2023 P] Ignore people who oppose your self-improvement out of fear you will outgrow them; growth makes you more useful to a healthy group or lets you break a toxic one {{Ux-tXsicqIk@3597}}
- [1v 2023 P] No one on social media is your competition; you are your own competition {{iU-oYc8fbEM@1139}}
- [1v 2024 P] Self-improvement pushes cluster in the cold months and around New Year because people see them as a fresh start; this always existed, social media just repackaged and hyped it {{z9MttM0Bq-I@65}}

## difference / between / biggest / ability (4)
- [1v 2022 P] Staying near 85% puts you in a flow state between anxiety (overtraining) and boredom (undertraining) {{z2uzpl_smSg@2587}}
- [1v 2022 P] The difference between those who make it and those who do not is belief in your ability to improve {{eCR3dcmCPV8@1188}}
- [1v 2023 P] Training makes the biggest difference to a physique, far more than the diet {{43F9unmSc_I@2200}}
- [1v 2023 P] The difference between intermediate and advanced is time and the decision to keep going {{16fjHEtZTCA@1965}}

## hinder / cope / drop / diminish (3)
- [1v 2020 O] Many lifters attach their ego, experience and reputation to a training standard; when it turns out to be bad, they cope rather than admit they were wrong, which is toxic and hinders progress. {{7DtKMEEua2g@0}}
- [1v 2020 O] The only thing that stops people is fear of what others will think; drop it and no one can hinder you {{Lg-w_lgCgUc@1570}}
- [1v 2022 O] Blaming diminishing returns is a cope advanced lifters use when progress stalls {{JgAoGDmM4n4@124}}

## assess / objectively / key / help (3)
- [1v 2020 P] Self-reflection, objectively assessing whether what you do helps you, is a key skill for bodybuilders and athletes. {{7DtKMEEua2g@126}}
- [1v 2022 P] The real key to hypertrophy is being able to keep training day in, day out for years, so embrace anything that helps with that {{BW-32tYoNxw@1200}}
- [1v 2022 P] Real humility is objectively assessing where you stand; saying 'I am strong' when you are strong is honesty, not arrogance {{7dDEDax8854@62}}

## speed / accomplish / appli / pursu (3)
- [1v 2020 P] Never falling behind applies to pursuing, not accomplishing: ignore others' talent, speed or your age (even starting at 35); never lose in effort and greatness eventually comes. {{1CZQe76Ty4k@1132}}
- [1v 2020 P] Will is applied to one thing at a time; single-minded focus on the present task lets you accomplish anything {{RLwjGoejUXk@757}}
- [1v 2024 P] Making yourself miserable doesn't speed progress; being happy and fulfilled while pursuing fitness raises your odds of success {{lGbT0aT2hOk@1111}}

## sadnes / coward / emotion / healthy (3)
- [1v 2020 P] Do not say things like 'I am a coward' or 'it hurts'; expressing real emotions like sadness is different and healthy {{1z494UcRwqo@189}}
- [1v 2022 P] Watch how someone reacts to confidence: a healthy man cultivates it, a coward resents it as arrogance {{nZnt4--nIpY@257}}
- [1v 2024 P] Using anger and sadness as training fuel keeps those emotions around instead of resolving them {{lGbT0aT2hOk@1300}}

## optimistic / believ / pessimistic / mindset (3)
- [1v 2020 O] Believing 'your time will come' is an optimistic mindset that always pays off; if you believe it never will, it never will {{xoxYiiTPdIs@150}}
- [1v 2021 P] Nobody deserves anything; you get what you work for, and believing you deserve things breeds a victim mindset {{TD_WzcWEmFg@376}}
- [1v 2023 P] Being active matters more than being optimistic or pessimistic {{Ux-tXsicqIk@2013}}

## tunnel / vision / lock / self-mastery (3)
- [1v 2020 P] Blacking out or tunnel vision in a fight is poor self-mastery from uncontrolled adrenaline; you miss attacks from the side {{xxOQxGwiX4I@941}}
- [1v 2021 P] Let your mind broaden as your frame does; tunnel vision makes you ignore good options that could replace bad ones in your arsenal {{I2aR7F5rCuE@2171}}
- [1v 2021 P] Refuse to be locked into one dogma; tunnel vision only helps if you happen to be heading in the right direction {{I2aR7F5rCuE@392}}

## nofap / abstinence / chronic / rebound (3)
- [1v 2021 P] The energy and confidence boost people feel on NoFap is mostly just recovery from chronic over-masturbation, not a real benefit of abstinence {{IPSRlANKpDw@1938}}
- [1v 2021 P] Neither chronic masturbation nor total abstinence works; NoFap and chronic masturbation are two halves of one cycle of streaks and relapses {{IPSRlANKpDw@2444}}
- [1v 2022 P] Fasting or abstinence only works if you understand why you are doing it; without that meaning it rebounds into gluttony or, as with NoFap, a sexual rebound {{AJ8AhxJWas8@2714}}

## fighter / number / compet / sub-priority (3)
- [1v 2022 P] For a fighter muscle building is a sub-priority; the number one priority is becoming a better fighter {{3JNwlqx3Z6w@227}}
- [1v 2022 O] Mocking a defeated fighter when you have never competed yourself is cowardice {{SDVF9vWBQAM@817}}
- [1v 2023 O] Environment matters: Dagestan's harsh culture plus wrestling produces a remarkable number of top fighters {{rFfLUloDHeg@689}}

## apathy / struggle / breed / docile (3)
- [1v 2022 P] Happiness is a byproduct of struggle; pursuing it directly breeds apathy and kills the will to pursue anything {{-PsAWpoj_ho@3553}}
- [1v 2022 P] Struggle tempers the soul; comfort keeps people fat and docile and makes apathy the norm {{wICZ5By6-2c@64}}
- [1v 2024 O] Isekai culture breeds apathy: waiting for something magical to change your fate instead of effort {{RPW-PxhYqrA@1386}}

## Possible contradictions / changes over time (25 flagged by script; verify)
- (stance flips) 2020-03: After two years of hard work while the prodigy did random things, NH's physique now surpasses his: hard work beats talent when talent doesn't work hard. {{aayHBXFiz7k@437}} {{n-n3txKjGmE@943}} ⟷ 2020-10: Work is the great equalizer: grinding beats talent over time, though a talented person who works just as hard stays ahead, so work harder {{mO3cqgBEqdc@888}}
- (stance flips) 2020-11: He listens to educational talks during warm-ups and volume work and keeps music only for hard sets, because that hypes him up more {{m1fD0HP6sIg@566}} {{CojMme7jr3o@311}} ⟷ 2021-05: Don't listen to music for the whole session, because you blunt its adrenaline boost; use podcasts or language lessons for warm-ups and save hard music for working sets {{QTHtVBP_36g@2618}}
- (stance flips) 2020-07: Motivation is fleeting, discipline is better, and the best state is not needing discipline because you love the training {{RPUhg61IV-g@0}} {{kKX0AUrXzIw@2199}} ⟷ 2021-07: Motivation is fleeting and gets you going; discipline keeps you going. Build it by imitating disciplined people {{m1fD0HP6sIg@1760}} {{8ELQ2XZgpls@444}}
- (stance flips) 2020-09: Scheduled days off make you eager to return, while impromptu skipped days breed more laziness {{Qgpc7evE7_4@884}} ⟷ 2021-05: Scheduled rest days are not skipped days; missing a scheduled training day means you were under-recovered from poor programming or your training is not fun {{0RoULYC_tao@0}}
- (stance flips) 2022-07: Doomers use the black pill as an excuse to give up and to justify misery they inflict on themselves {{_KT_9vmHNzE@312}} ⟷ 2024-09: Looks matter, but the black pill is an emotional excuse for passivity; never give up {{jmOPC_ozpwc@9026}}
- (stance flips) 2020-09: An unbreakable spirit needs an unbreakable body, built by training on a disciplined schedule {{Qgpc7evE7_4@1011}} ⟷ 2021-01: Training every day, while not the most optimal for muscle, builds unbreakable discipline by making lifting part of your schedule like lunch or sleep {{ivrGNRW0rwE@501}}
- (stance flips) 2021-05: Comparison is the thief of joy; there is no point comparing yourself with others unless you compete with them directly, which never happens on YouTube fitness {{_pbYAfyNM0c@1323}} ⟷ 2023-10: Comparison is the thief of joy; despite a very good body he has at times been unable to enjoy it because of comparison {{iU-oYc8fbEM@1076}}
- (stance flips) 2020-04: 'From one thing, ten thousand things': don't just absorb information but infer further ideas from it, and don't become a fanboy who treats one idea as gospel, including his own videos. {{KadH0ZYObS0@188}} ⟷ 2023-06: Dogmatism attaches the ego to one idea; instead, from one thing know ten thousand things {{LGmtei80ohU@1067}}
- (stance flips) 2022-12: The difference between those who make it and those who do not is belief in your ability to improve {{eCR3dcmCPV8@1188}} ⟷ 2023-10: The difference between intermediate and advanced is time and the decision to keep going {{16fjHEtZTCA@1965}}
- (stance flips) 2020-09: Someone will always be better, sometimes without working for it; complaining is a loser's mindset, focus only on what you do {{vZUEtun_5no@635}} ⟷ 2021-02: Dreaming and high hopes are always better than a blackpill loser's mindset; if you do not believe in it, you will not work hard or progress {{YHm-qSHgGSk@62}}
- (stance flips) 2020-11: For natural bodybuilding, being realistic just means low standards; realistic people stop pushing once they hit their limit {{xxOQxGwiX4I@375}} ⟷ 2024-12: Expectations do not need to be realistic; standards tied to what you know you can accomplish are by definition low {{uKOuj3Mzc9c@1070}}
- (stance flips) 2023-07: Calf training is only boring if you don't push it hard; train to a burning pump and you'll start to enjoy it {{6KA80BQ9W3k@943}} ⟷ 2024-11: The fear of being boring is worse than being boring; be passionate and enjoy yourself {{o40xCjIzl14@7841}}
- (stance flips) 2020-11: Bigorexia has no patience; even if you reach the dream body the mind immediately wants more, so the dream body is a mirage {{MuQvx5dNpno@596}} ⟷ 2022-08: Hold to your principles and dreams even against good counterarguments; if someone can talk you out of a dream, you never had one {{ilGY8qFjuxI@662}} {{WdFEEkLfBRc@1446}}
- (stance flips) 2020-07: Envy becomes a sin when it turns into jealousy and resentment toward the people who have what you want {{zt2-wI7GJ-c@125}} ⟷ 2021-09: Admiration makes you want to copy someone and pushes you to act; envy is resentment from believing you will never have what they have, and it keeps you from working on it {{Da6d5cecRKA@103}}
- (stance flips) 2020-09: Like bodybuilding, more stress handled means more growth: the more training stress you can handle, the bigger and stronger you get {{24j6eNqmnZ0@1260}} ⟷ 2021-02: Not training your body is a sign of hatred, not love; it was engineered to handle constant stress {{TPPCu5zBPLk@490}}
- (stance flips) 2020-10: Being a serious lifter starts at around 8-10 hours a week in the gym, enough that training replaces hobbies or social time {{kh--k3genZA@755}} ⟷ 2024-09: Training about 5 hours a week needs no lifestyle changes, it replaces Netflix and Instagram time, but do not center your whole life around the gym; that will not make you happy {{bRKQIOYo2u4@843}}
- (stance flips) 2021-11: The greater the distance between you and your physique role model, the worse the dysmorphia; enhanced influencers keep growing while their natural audience does not {{Tos7dHreF3k@553}} ⟷ 2022-07: Virtuous role models have been removed for young men, leaving influencers barely older than their audience {{zAh18SRUOLw@3974}}
- (stance flips) 2021-10: Hold to your principles and dreams even against good counterarguments; if someone can talk you out of a dream, you never had one {{ilGY8qFjuxI@662}} {{WdFEEkLfBRc@1446}} ⟷ 2023-09: A finite dream disappoints once reached; an evolving, unreasonable dream keeps you going {{hechG8y7Y1I@1764}}
- (stance flips) 2020-11: Motivation is fleeting and gets you going; discipline keeps you going. Build it by imitating disciplined people {{m1fD0HP6sIg@1760}} {{8ELQ2XZgpls@444}} ⟷ 2024-11: External motivation is never sustainable; motivation content fills people with empty motivation and keeps them from building discipline {{z9MttM0Bq-I@380}}
- (stance flips) 2021-03: The wrong thing done consistently beats the right thing done sparingly: a kid doing daily chin-ups and push-ups at the park beats someone running a perfect paid program every two weeks {{_L5wLHlFfI0@125}} {{he2KJeMcoAI@2144}} ⟷ 2024-01: Consistency beats theory: a guy doing park push-ups and pull-ups with a rock backpack will outdo a theory-perfect lifter who can't stick to it {{jDk3LytTEjA@2778}}
- (stance flips) 2022-09: Mindset matters more than method {{g9YniHngWDI@1790}} ⟷ 2023-10: Copy the mindset of elite naturals, not their methods, which may not fit your body or lifestyle {{iU-oYc8fbEM@0}}
- (stance flips) 2020-02: What matters most for a novice is building the discipline of coming in three times a week; motivation doesn't work in bodybuilding, routine does. {{_4wWgin1dVY@379}} ⟷ 2021-08: Turn motivation into discipline and novelty into routine {{9mnLRQsrs9k@3268}}
- (stance flips) 2021-04: Over-focusing on diet gives subpar results; training is the most important thing {{XASWAdsSu0M@1207}} ⟷ 2022-03: Over-focusing on results makes people ignore the journey and never reach the results; the results are the by-product of doing it for the difficulty {{ZPBwBrOktgA@192}}
- (stance flips) 2020-07: Hate aimed at someone who does not know you exist would make you far happier if spent lifting {{zt2-wI7GJ-c@248}} ⟷ 2023-06: Much of the hate aimed at Asian men, like hate aimed at short men, comes from within the group itself: self-hatred that accepts and amplifies stereotypes {{wE3vuAA7K1Y@566}}
- (stance flips) 2020-12: Ideals were never meant to be realistic; aiming for a manga physique is fine {{WyxeMHovPJ8@830}} ⟷ 2023-02: Your ideal physique should be unattainable; a realistic goal yields less effort and less result {{UWr5TQVT63g@751}}
